- **abraham-lincoln**: 16th President who preserved the Union through the Civil War and emancipated slaves while embodying moral leadership and democratic ideals ([leamas-ai/leamas.sh](https://github.com/leamas-ai/leamas.sh/blob/HEAD/kits/agents/agents-historical-figures/agents/abraham-lincoln-agent.md) · ★15)
- **absence-check**: Fresh-context reviewer for CLAIMS OF ABSENCE in work just done. Use at a gate, before a commit, before reporting a finding, or when asked to check a session's claims. Hand it a diff, a commit range, or a list of claims; it answers one mechanical question — which "X is not there" claims were verified by a search shown capable of finding X. It does NOT assess how an agent is doing, and it does not watch anything; it reads artefacts. Invoke it; it cannot observe. ([lotusboy/bakehouse](https://github.com/lotusboy/bakehouse/blob/HEAD/agents/absence-check.md))
- **absence-mapper**: Verifies that a candidate idea has no production implementation. A thorough absence proof is what turns a hypothesis into a genuine frontier finding. ([Nerfherder16/BrickLayer](https://github.com/Nerfherder16/BrickLayer/blob/HEAD/template-frontier/.claude/agents/absence-mapper.md))
- **absolute-architect**: Decomposes complex engineering requirements into granular SPIDR micro-tasks (Spike, Prototype, Interface, Data, Rules) with a strict dependency DAG, exact target files and executable acceptance tests. Use for multi-file features, new projects, or any work too large for one focused change. Read-only — produces plans, never implementation. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-architect.md))
- **absolute-backend**: Council seat — senior backend engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns APIs, data model and storage, transactions and consistency, queues and background jobs, integrations (payments, delivery, messaging), idempotency, caching and failure handling; attacks proposals that lose data, double-charge, race, or cannot be operated. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-backend.md))
- **absolute-chair**: Council chair for /absolute-council and /absolute-goal - reads the brief, blind proposals, anonymised critiques, revisions, weighted scores, the devil's advocate and the orchestrator's tool-verified facts in fresh context; decides by evidence and the weighted rubric (never by vote count, seniority or eloquence), assembles the decision from the strongest credited parts, records dissent with revisit triggers, and says whether the council converged, needs exactly one more round, or must escalate a values call to the user. Read-only; returns the decision text. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-chair.md))
- **absolute-code-mapper**: Read-only codebase reconnaissance before implementation — finds reusable utilities, types, schemas, conventions, design tokens and existing components so new code reuses instead of duplicating. Use at the start of any feature or refactor in an existing repository. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-code-mapper.md))
- **absolute-contrarian**: Council seat and devil's advocate. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal it questions the premise (are we solving the right problem?), runs pre-mortems and inversions, and in round R4 attacks the current leading proposal in at most 200 words, naming the single fact that would flip the verdict. Seated when the decision has no natural challenger or the council agrees too fast. Read-only; returns its output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-contrarian.md))
- **absolute-debugger**: Systematic debugger for the FIX pipeline - turns a bug report, failing test or error into a deterministic red command, minimises it, tests falsifiable hypotheses, names the root cause with file:line, applies the minimal fix and proves it with a revert-red / restore-green regression test. Use for bugs, regressions after upgrades, flaky tests and unexplained errors; not for new features. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-debugger.md))
- **absolute-design-critic**: Independent, read-only design critic for built UI. Reviews screenshots first (blind to the builder's reasoning), then code - anti-slop pre-flight, design-lint, Nielsen heuristics, cognitive load, persona walkthroughs, DESIGN.md adherence. Returns one verdict word (SHIP, FIX, REBUILD, RECAPTURE) with at most 8 ranked, concrete fixes. Use after a UI build or redesign, before calling design work done. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-design-critic.md))
- **absolute-devops**: Council seat — platform and reliability engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns hosting, CI/CD, environments, secrets, observability (logs, metrics, errors, uptime), performance under load, backups, cost and the release/rollback path; attacks proposals that cannot be deployed, monitored, rolled back or afforded. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-devops.md))
- **absolute-frontend**: Council seat — senior frontend engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the client side - framework and rendering strategy (SSR, SSG, SPA, islands), routing, state and data fetching, component boundaries, forms, performance budgets (LCP, INP, CLS, bundle size), accessibility in markup, i18n and browser support; attacks proposals that are slow, fragile or impossible to maintain in the browser. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-frontend.md))
- **absolute-implementer**: TDD GREEN phase — writes the minimal, production-grade code that makes the failing tests pass, under the Ponytail anti-overengineering protocol (YAGNI, reuse, stdlib first, minimal diff, never negligent). Use after absolute-test-engineer for each task, and for fix rounds after review. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-implementer.md))
- **absolute-mobile**: Council seat — mobile app engineer (iOS, Android, React Native/Expo, Flutter). In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns native vs cross-platform choice, offline and sync, push notifications, deep links, permissions, device performance and battery, app-store rules and release mechanics; attacks proposals that ignore platform conventions, store review or bad networks. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-mobile.md))
- **absolute-product**: Council seat — product lead. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the user problem, scope, MVP cut, success metrics, release criteria and what NOT to build; attacks proposals that add cost without user value or ship something nobody asked for. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-product.md))
- **absolute-reference-hunter**: Gathers 3–6 real-world design references for a brief in its own context (keeps the designer's context clean) - picks sources from the PPP library, uses MCP servers when connected, fetches or browses, and writes a cited Reference Brief to .design/references.md. Use at the start of the DESIGN and CREATIVE pipelines or whenever references are needed. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-reference-hunter.md))
- **absolute-reviewer**: Independent read-only code critic for a diff — functional correctness against the spec, test quality, Ponytail minimalism (no bloat, no speculative abstraction), naming, error handling, and time/space complexity. Returns PASS, REVISE or BLOCK with line-level fixes. Use after each implementation task, in parallel with the security auditor. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-reviewer.md))
- **absolute-security-auditor**: Read-only security gate for a diff — injection (SQL/NoSQL/command/XSS/path traversal), authN/authZ flaws and IDOR, secrets and PII leaks, weak crypto, SSRF, unsafe deserialization, prototype pollution, ReDoS and missing limits (OWASP Top 10 / CWE Top 25). Returns PASS or BLOCK with exact remediations. Use after each implementation task and before merging. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-security-auditor.md))
- **absolute-test-engineer**: Strict TDD RED phase — writes failing unit/integration tests that define a task's acceptance boundary (happy path, boundaries, negative cases, concurrency) before any implementation exists, and proves they fail for the right reason. Use as the first step of each implementation task. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-test-engineer.md))
- **absolute-ux**: Council seat — UX and interaction designer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns flows, information architecture, navigation, forms, states (loading, empty, error, success), microcopy, accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA), mobile ergonomics and cognitive load; attacks proposals that add steps, hide status, or make users think. Brings real-world references for flows. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-ux.md))
- **absolute-verifier**: Release gate for integrated work — runs the full test suite, typecheck, lint and production build, boots the app and checks runtime logs, and (for UI changes) runs multi-viewport visual checks. Never trusts claims without command output. Use at the end of a feature or before declaring a goal complete. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-verifier.md))
- **absolute-visual**: Council seat — art director and visual designer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the visual direction - concept, typography (incl. Cyrillic), colour with verified contrast, layout and rhythm, imagery, motion and brand fit - and brings real references from the design library; attacks generic, template-looking, AI-looking proposals and visual ideas that cannot be built well. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-visual.md))
- **absorber**: Evaluate whether an external project or capability can be absorbed by the receiving system. ([GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent](https://github.com/GodSpeedAI/GodSpeed-Agent/blob/HEAD/agents/absorber.md))
- **absorption-check**: Warm per-round absorption check for `design.md` and track files: two-way coverage match between the research log's load-bearing decisions and the draft's decision records, not readability judgment. - **ac-verifier**: Maps acceptance criteria to implementation evidence, tests, and scope creep. Use during review when a diff, PR, ticket, or story includes numbered ACs. ([HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard](https://github.com/HoangNguyen0403/agent-skills-standard/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/ac-verifier.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-verifier**: Use as the final gate before a build (or a build phase) is called done. Route here after the build and verify-acs have run and the builder claims every acceptance criterion for the phase is met, or whenever someone is about to tick off, close, or ship against docs/prd/acceptance-checklist.md. This is the independent evidence check, not a builder or fixer. Do NOT route here mid-build, for writing tests, or for open-ended "is my code good" reviews. ([jiffi-co/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jiffi-co/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/builder-kit/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Mechanical gate for ROADMAP acceptance criteria. Runs `cmd:` and `invariant:` ACs plus the Harness Build, Test, Lint, and Invariants commands against the current working tree or a worktree, and reports pass/fail per command. No edit tools. Does not judge behavior; `probe:` ACs belong to @validator. Used for per-task AC checks and per-phase integration checks. ([JonFermin/vibecoding](https://github.com/JonFermin/vibecoding/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Independent acceptance-criteria verifier for factory station S10. Given only a story's acceptance criteria, the diff and the project's test commands, it re-runs the tests and returns pass, fail or not-verifiable for each criterion, with evidence. It never changes code. ([Nandan-krishnamurthy/ai-software-factory](https://github.com/Nandan-krishnamurthy/ai-software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Verifies a pull request's acceptance criteria in the running app and records each result with evidence. Use it in reviews with delegated checks, once the criteria are formulated. It drives the shared browser, so never run it at the same time as another browser subagent. ([StadtLandNetz/Claudio](https://github.com/StadtLandNetz/Claudio/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-verifier**: Use to verify a feature's acceptance criteria and report a per-AC pass/fail status. The verification method is set by ac_verification_mode: observe the running app (browser/API) under runtime-assisted, run the test suite under tests, or read code under code-only or off. Use proactively after implementation when a spec's AC items need verification; runtime-assisted requires the app to be running. ([webmint/mintEnvoy](https://github.com/webmint/mintEnvoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **ac-verifier**: Independent verifier of a specforge spec's acceptance criteria. Give it the spec id and the commit sha to verify, never the author's reasoning. It runs every criterion's verify target itself, writes the evidence file, and returns a results table. It never fixes anything. ([zalmb8/specforge](https://github.com/zalmb8/specforge/blob/HEAD/agents/ac-verifier.md))
- **ac-writer**: Product Team acceptance-criteria seat - fills each story's Scenarios row with the PRD scenario ids that slice satisfies, and reports any slice needing a criterion no scenario covers. Use ONLY from /5-decompose with the PRD path and the story files; it never invents a requirement or a scenario. ([jmanuelrosa/agent-catalog](https://github.com/jmanuelrosa/agent-catalog/blob/HEAD/bundles/product-team/agents/ac-writer.md))
- **ac2-security-reviewer**: Reviews AC2 protocol code (frame parser, CRC-16-CCITT, HMAC-SHA-256, replay protection) against the IRS and SR-* requirements. Use proactively on any diff that touches UART framing, crypto, or the gateway bridge. Not yet relevant — the parser does not exist in the repo yet (as of Phase 1a). ([oruccakir/AegisCore](https://github.com/oruccakir/AegisCore/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ac2-security-reviewer.md))
- **aca-report-agent**: Writes the results summary of an Android cleanup run from the CLI's own numbers: before/after RAM and storage, what was removed, turned off and changed, what was skipped or rolled back and why, and how to undo it. ([willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent](https://github.com/willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aca-report-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **aca-risk-judge-agent**: Independent risk judge. Reviews the approved change list of an Android cleanup run right before it is applied, and vetoes items that look risky for this user (a feature they said they use, a high-risk change without a clear reason, an app that looks like one of their main apps). It can only remove items from the approved set; it never approves or adds anything. ([willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent](https://github.com/willcoliveira/android-cleanup-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aca-risk-judge-agent.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
